## ⚙️ Auto-Greeting Setup Guide

### Step 1 — Import n8n Workflow
1. Open your n8n instance (Hostinger VPS)
2. Click **Import Workflow**
3. Upload `n8n_all_stores_workflow.json`
4. Activate the workflow

### Step 2 — Connect Credentials
| Service | Credential | Used For |
|---|---|---|
| Gmail | countrycoveinc@gmail.com | Send emails for all stores |
| Twilio | Long Island 631/516 number | WhatsApp messages |
| Odoo | country-cove-inc.odoo.com | Log activity to CRM |

### Step 3 — Schedule
- Workflow runs **daily at 9:00 AM EST**
- Fires at **7 days before**, **3 days before**, **1 day before**, and **day-of**
- Each store gets its own branded message

### Step 4 — Odoo Homepage Greetings
- Run `inject_all_store_greetings.py` to push celebration banners to all 7 Odoo websites
- Banners auto-update based on the current date

| File | Description |
|---|---|
| `CELEBRATIONS.md` | This file — full calendar |

| `email_whatsapp_templates.xlsx` | Ready-to-send templates |
| `celebration_templates.json` | n8n-compatible JSON |
| `n8n_all_stores_workflow.json` | Import into n8n |
| `inject_all_store_greetings.py` | Push greetings to all 7 Odoo homepages |
